## 55. Track Your Progress Missing After Registering a Mission Late, While Already on Earn Stage

Bug report, with exact repro steps: register for Spread the Word only, complete that mission (advancing Register → Engage → Earn), then — now sitting on Earn stage — register Bank More with Us via Available Missions. Engage-stage merging (unlocking "Upgrade to Priority Banking"/"Open a New Investment Account" into Personalized Offers) worked correctly, but Earn stage stayed stuck showing only "Mission Completed Spread the Word" — Bank More's own "Track Your Progress" never appeared.

**Root cause, two parts.** First, no merge function existed for this case at all: `getDiegoEngageOffersForMission` (§49/§50) merges a newly-registered mission's Engage actions into Personalized Offers when they're not the current stage's own fetch, and `getDiegoNewlyCompletedMissionOffers` (§53) does the same for a Mission Completed card — but nothing did it for Track Your Progress itself. Second, once that merge function was added, it still returned `null` on first try: Track Your Progress had already been auto-resolved to a stale `"dismissed"` by `applyPriorityBankingCampaignAdjustments`'s Earn-stage branch during Diego's very first (pre-registration) view of Earn stage, back when Bank More wasn't registered yet — the same "premature auto-resolve" bug class fixed repeatedly elsewhere in this campaign (§49, §50), just not yet covered for this specific card.

**Fix, `js/cdh.js`**: added `getDiegoTrackProgressOfferForMission(missionTitle, session, customer)`, mirroring `getDiegoEngageOffersForMission`/`getDiegoNewlyCompletedMissionOffers` — returns the mission's "Track Your Progress" (or "...for Spread the Word") card at 0% Complete if that mission is registered, not yet complete, and not already genuinely responded, first clearing a stale `"dismissed"` entry exactly like `clearStaleMissionEngageResolve`/`clearStaleMissionCompletedResolve` already do. Wired into `dashboard.html`'s `handleRespond`, alongside the existing Engage-merge call, whenever a response comes in via Available Missions.

**Related regression caught proactively**: the pre-existing "refresh Track Your Progress in place" logic in `handleRespond` (from §50, before §54's hide-on-complete rule existed) only relabeled the card's percentage via `.map()` — it never removed the card once its mission finished, which would have shown a frozen "100% Complete" Track Your Progress card side-by-side with the newly merged Mission Completed card, violating §54. Converted it to `.filter().map()`: filter out a Track Your Progress card once its mission is complete, then relabel whatever's left.

**Verified live**: fresh Diego session, registered Spread the Word only, completed it via Refer Your Buddy (confirmed `currentStageIndex: 3`, Earn), reproduced the bug exactly as reported (Personalized Offers showed only "Mission Completed Spread the Word"), then registered Bank More with Us via Available Missions — Personalized Offers immediately updated to also show "Track your Progress — 0% Complete — tracking your Bank More with Us mission." alongside the two newly merged Engage cards and the existing Mission Completed Spread the Word card. No console errors.

---

## 56. Track Your Progress → Mission Completed Transition Broken Specifically for Bank More with Us (Investment Application Wizard Bypasses Diego's Post-Response Logic)

Immediate follow-up report after §55: "Track your progress works for spread the word but not for Bank more with us." Continued the same live session from §55 — accepted "Upgrade to Priority Banking" (Track Your Progress correctly advanced to 50%), then completed "Open a New Investment Account" via its own multi-step wizard (`investment-application.html`) and returned to the dashboard. Personalized Offers still showed the stale "Mission Completed Spread the Word" only — no "Mission Completed" for Bank More, and (worse than §55's bug) no way to reach it at all, since Track Your Progress had also vanished with nothing replacing it.

**Root cause**: "Open a New Investment Account" isn't accepted through `dashboard.html`'s own `handleRespond` like a normal offer card — clicking Apply Now (`handleApplyInvestmentAccount`) defers the real response to `investment-application.html`'s own `handleFinalize`, which calls `respondToOffer` directly once the wizard completes, then redirects back to `dashboard.html` as a **fresh page load**. That fresh load runs `loadOffers()`, not `handleRespond` — and `clearStaleMissionCompletedResolve` (§53) was only ever called from inside `handleRespond`'s Diego block, never from `loadOffers()`. So the stale `"dismissed"` that `applyPriorityBankingCampaignAdjustments` had written onto "Mission Completed" during an earlier (pre-completion) Earn-stage view was never cleared — and `loadOffers()`'s own `matched.filter(o => journeyActionResponses[title] === undefined)` line excludes any title with a non-`undefined` response *before* `applyPriorityBankingCampaignAdjustments` even runs, so "Mission Completed" for Bank More could never reach the screen no matter what the campaign-adjustment filter said. "Refer Your Buddy" (Spread the Word's own completing action) never hit this gap because it's accepted directly via a normal `handleRespond` call on the dashboard, which already clears that staleness.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: added a `clearStaleMissionCompletedResolve(session)` call inside `loadOffers()`'s Diego branch, right after `ensureDiegoContainerOffers` and before the `matched.filter(...)` line that excludes already-responded titles — so any stale "dismissed" on a Mission Completed title is cleared before that filter runs, regardless of which code path (direct `handleRespond`, or a wizard's own deferred `respondToOffer` landing back on a fresh page load) produced the completing response.

**Verified live**: continued the same session — after adding the fix, finalized the investment application wizard for real (filled + submitted, `handleFinalize` ran, "Open a New Investment Account accepted" logged) and returned to `dashboard.html` via a genuine fresh navigation (not a SPA re-render). Personalized Offers correctly showed both "Mission Completed Spread the Word" and "Mission Completed" (Bank More with Us), with Track Your Progress correctly gone from both. No console errors.

---

## 57. Accepting One "Mission Completed" Card Made the Other One (And the Scratch Cards It Unlocks) Unreachable

Bug report: when both missions happen to be complete at once (so both "Mission Completed" and "Mission Completed Spread the Word" are showing together in Personalized Offers, per §56), clicking one makes the other disappear — because accepting a Mission Completed card also advances the journey stage straight from Earn to Scratch & Win (`maybeAdvanceJourneyStage`'s Earn-stage rule), and that stage advance triggers a fresh `loadOffers()` that replaces `dashState.offers` wholesale with Scratch & Win's own fetch. The still-unresponded sibling Mission Completed card — which belongs to Earn, a stage Diego just left behind — has no way back once that happens.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: inside `handleRespond`'s "stage advanced" branch (added by §55/§56's own edits), after `await loadOffers()` re-merge any Mission Completed card whose mission is complete but still unresponded via `getDiegoNewlyCompletedMissionOffers` — same function §53 already uses elsewhere, just now also called on this path, since it correctly returns the sibling regardless of which stage the customer is currently viewing.

**A second-order break found live while verifying the fix**: with the sibling now reachable and click-able again, accepting it (Bank More's "Mission Completed", after "Mission Completed Spread the Word" had already been accepted first and advanced the stage) revealed only "Your Third Scratch Card" — the two scratch cards Bank More's own Mission Completed unlocks ("This Is Your First Scratch Card", "Your Second Scratch Card, Keep It Up") never appeared, because accepting that re-merged card doesn't touch Scratch & Win's own fetch either (it isn't a Scratch & Win title, so `maybeAdvanceJourneyStage`'s `isCurrentStageOffer` check is false and nothing re-fetches) — so those two cards stayed auto-resolved `"dismissed"` from whenever Scratch & Win was first viewed, before Bank More was complete. The same gap, one layer further in, also affects the reward tile a scratch card unlocks (e.g. "500 Points Earned" after scratching card one) — `applyPriorityBankingCampaignAdjustments`'s Scratch & Win branch auto-resolves a reward tile at the same time as its scratch card (deliberately, to avoid the exact premature-lock bug this is — see the comment at that branch), so it also needs an explicit re-merge once its own gate (the scratch card) actually gets accepted.

**Fix, `js/cdh.js`**: added `getDiegoUnlockedScratchOffers(session, customer)` — same shape as `getDiegoNewlyCompletedMissionOffers`, covering both scratch cards (gated on `Mission Completed`/`Mission Completed Spread the Word` being accepted) and reward tiles (gated on their own scratch card being accepted), clearing any stale `"dismissed"` auto-resolve before checking eligibility. Wired into `dashboard.html`'s `handleRespond`, in the same always-runs block as `getDiegoNewlyCompletedMissionOffers`, right after it.

**Verified live**, no reloads between steps (to genuinely exercise the runtime merge path rather than a fresh fetch that would mask the bug): registered both missions, completed both (Bank More's two Engage actions plus Refer Your Buddy), reloaded once to land on Earn stage with both Mission Completed cards showing together, then — without reloading again — accepted "Mission Completed Spread the Word" (stage advanced to Scratch & Win, "Mission Completed" correctly survived, still showing), accepted "Mission Completed" (all three scratch cards correctly appeared, no stage change since it isn't a Scratch & Win title), then scratched "This Is Your First Scratch Card" ("500 points Earned" correctly appeared). No console errors at any step.

---

## 58. Scratch Cards Get a "Scratch" Button, and Reveal Their Prize In Place Instead of as a Separate Offer

Two related requests: scratch card titles should show a "Scratch" button instead of the generic "View offer" (a byproduct of `mapPegaOffer` always setting `cta: "View offer"` on live-container offers, unaware of what any title actually means in this campaign); and scratching a card should reveal what it won *inside that same card*, the way a real scratch card works, rather than the reward appearing as a second, separate "500 Points Earned"-style offer elsewhere in Personalized Offers.

**CTA fix, `js/cdh.js`**: added the three scratch titles to the existing CTA-override mechanism — but doing that surfaced a real gap: the override table (`ctaOverrides`) was local to `applyPriorityBankingCampaignAdjustments`, only ever applied to offers passing through its own stage-scoped fetch. Every merge function this campaign has accumulated since §49 (`getDiegoEngageOffersForMission`, `getDiegoNewlyCompletedMissionOffers`, `getDiegoUnlockedScratchOffers`, etc.) hydrates its offers through `getDiegoContainerOfferByTitle` instead — a completely separate path the override never touched. Confirmed live: a scratch card that arrived via one of §57's merges showed "View offer" while one from a fresh stage fetch correctly showed "Scratch." Fixed by hoisting the table to a module-scoped `DIEGO_CTA_OVERRIDES` constant and applying it inside `getDiegoContainerOfferByTitle` itself, so every consumer gets the right label regardless of path; `applyPriorityBankingCampaignAdjustments` now just references the same shared constant instead of keeping its own copy.

**In-place reveal, `js/cdh.js` + `dashboard.html`**: added `DIEGO_SCRATCH_REWARD_TITLE` (maps each scratch card title to the reward title it unlocks) and `getDiegoScratchRevealDescription(scratchCardTitle, session)`, which pulls the matching reward's own description straight from the cached container response and returns it prefixed `"🎉 Revealed! ..."`. Wired into `dashboard.html`'s `handleRespond`: when a scratch card is accepted, its paired reward is still accepted for real via its own `respondToOffer` call (so Interaction History and Scratch & Win's "every title needs a response" stage-advancement rule both stay correct) — but instead of letting the generic "remove the responded offer from view" behavior fire, the scratch card is mutated in place (its `description` swapped for the reveal text) and left in `dashState.offers`, so `renderOfferCard`'s existing "Accepted" badge (driven by `responses[offer.id]`, already set by the time this runs) takes over automatically — no new rendering path needed. `getDiegoUnlockedScratchOffers` (§57) was simplified to stop returning reward tiles altogether, since they're never meant to be their own visible card anymore.

**Verified live**, no reloads between individual scratches: registered and completed both missions, reached Earn with both Mission Completed cards, accepted them in sequence (all three scratch cards correctly appeared with "Scratch" buttons, per §57's fix), then scratched each one in turn — the first two revealed their prize in place ("🎉 Revealed! 500 points Earned" / "🎉 Revealed! 2000 points Earned") with an "Accepted" badge and no separate reward card anywhere, and the third correctly advanced the stage straight to Fulfil (all six Scratch & Win titles now responded) with Fulfil's own two offers showing. No console errors at any step.

---

## 59. Third Scratch Card's Reveal Was Invisible — the Same Stage-Advance-Wipes-the-Card Bug, One More Time

Direct follow-up to §58: scratching the *third* card (the one that completes every Scratch & Win title at once, per §58's own test) advances the stage straight to Fulfil in the same response — and that stage advance's `loadOffers()` call replaces `dashState.offers` wholesale with Fulfil's own fetch, same as §57's Mission Completed bug, before Diego ever got to see what he'd won. `scratchReveal` (§58) was computed correctly but only ever consumed in the "stage didn't change" branch — the "stage advanced" branch never looked at it at all.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: in `handleRespond`'s stage-advanced branch, right alongside §57's own Mission Completed re-merge (same `if (customer.id === "C1004")` block, after `await loadOffers()`), push the just-scratched card back in with its revealed description whenever `scratchReveal` is set — `{ ...offer, description: scratchReveal }` — so it shows up appended to whichever stage he landed on (still tagged with its own "SCRATCH & WIN STAGE" badge, same as every other cross-stage merge in this campaign) rather than vanishing. Deliberately does NOT try to keep this specific card persisted across a genuine page reload — same as every other merge in this campaign, it's a live, in-session courtesy so he can still see the prize right after scratching, not a change to what a fresh fetch of Fulfil would normally return.

**Verified live**: fresh Diego session, registered and completed both missions, reached Earn with both Mission Completed cards, accepted both, then scratched all three cards in one uninterrupted sequence with no reloads. The third scratch correctly advanced `currentStageIndex` to 5 (Fulfil) and Personalized Offers showed Fulfil's own two offers *plus* "Your Third Scratch Card, Looks Like You're a Pro" — description "🎉 Revealed! You Have a 3% Cashback Voucher on Travel Spends," Accepted badge, no separate reward card. No console errors.

## 60. Scratch Card 1/2 Could Appear Out of Order; Discover's CTA Renamed to "Play Game"

Two requests. First, straightforward: the Discover-stage offer ("Register to Win Exciting Gifts") should say "Play Game" instead of the generic "View offer" — added to `DIEGO_CTA_OVERRIDES` (js/cdh.js, §58), same mechanism as every other themed CTA in this campaign.

Second: Scratch Card 1 was sometimes rendering *after* Card 2 in Personalized Offers. Root cause — `filterOffersForStage` (js/data.js) returns offers in whatever order they arrive in the live container response, which reflects Pega's own real-time arbitration ranking (score-based), not this campaign's hand-designed narrative sequence. That's fine, even desirable, for Robert's/Aria's journeys, where ranking IS the point of the demo — but Diego's Scratch & Win stage is a fixed story (card 1, then 2, then 3), so trusting arbitration order there was never correct to begin with. It surfaced concretely via §57's cross-stage merge path: when Bank More's Mission Completed unlocks both Card 1 and Card 2 in the same `getDiegoUnlockedScratchOffers` call, the live container's own response order (not narrative order) determined which one the `.filter().map()` chain produced first.

**Fix, `js/cdh.js`**: added `sortDiegoScratchCards(offers)` — a stable *partial* sort against a hand-authored `SCRATCH_CARD_ORDER` array, not a full re-sort of the offers list: every slot in the array currently occupied by one of the three scratch titles gets refilled in the correct relative order, while every other offer (including a cross-stage merge like a leftover Mission Completed card, §57) stays in exactly the position it already had. Wired in at every point `dashState.offers` can end up containing scratch cards: the tail of `applyPriorityBankingCampaignAdjustments` itself (covers any fresh stage-scoped fetch), the end of `handleRespond`'s main C1004 block in `dashboard.html` (covers `getDiegoUnlockedScratchOffers` merges), and the stage-advanced branch (covers §57's Mission Completed re-merge and §59's scratch-reveal append landing on Fulfil).

**Verified live**: confirmed "Play Game" renders on the Discover-stage card. Then reproduced the exact sequence that used to misorder cards — registered and completed both missions, reached Earn with both Mission Completed cards, accepted "Mission Completed Spread the Word" first (advancing to Scratch & Win with only Card 3 eligible from the fresh fetch), then "Mission Completed" (merging Cards 1 and 2 in via §57's path) — Personalized Offers now correctly read Card 1, Card 2, Card 3 in that order. Continued scratching all three in sequence to confirm no regression: reveals, CTA labels, and the final stage advance to Fulfil (with Card 3's reveal appended, §59) all still worked correctly. No console errors at any step.

---

## 61. Scratch & Win Could Advance to Fulfil Having Only Ever Revealed Two of Three Prizes

Direct request: only move to Fulfil once both missions are complete *and* all three scratch cards have actually been scratched. Previously, Scratch & Win had no Diego-specific stage-advance rule at all — it fell through to the generic "every one of this stage's six titles has SOME response" rule (`maybeAdvanceJourneyStage`'s `else` branch). That rule is satisfied by an auto-resolved `"dismissed"`, not just a genuine accept — and if only one mission ever gets completed (say Bank More with Us, never Spread the Word), Card 3 and its reward permanently auto-resolve to `"dismissed"` (`applyPriorityBankingCampaignAdjustments`, js/cdh.js, §51/§57) the moment Scratch & Win is first viewed. That's a real, recorded response — but never a revealed prize — and the generic rule would have happily advanced Diego to Fulfil having only ever scratched two of his three cards.

**Fix, `js/session.js`**: added a Diego-specific `stage.stage === "Scratch & Win"` branch to `maybeAdvanceJourneyStage`, alongside the existing Register/Engage/Earn special cases. `shouldAdvance` now requires `status === "accepted"` (a dismiss never advances) AND both missions genuinely complete (`bankMoreComplete`, `spreadWordComplete` — same checks used elsewhere in this campaign) AND all three scratch titles specifically `"accepted"` in `journeyActionResponses`, regardless of which particular title (a scratch card or its auto-accepted reward) triggered this specific check.

**Verified live** (on a fresh port after catching a stale-cache false failure — see below): registered and completed only Bank More with Us, scratched Cards 1 and 2 — confirmed `currentStageIndex` stayed at 4 (Scratch & Win), both cards showing their reveals correctly, no premature advance. Then registered and completed Spread the Word too (merging "Mission Completed Spread the Word" in via §57's path), accepted it (correctly unlocking Card 3, still stage 4), and scratched Card 3 — `currentStageIndex` advanced to 5 (Fulfil) only at that point, with Card 3's reveal correctly appended per §59. No console errors.

**Caught a false failure from stale cached JS mid-verification**: the very first live run of this fix appeared to fail — scratching just Cards 1 and 2 (one mission only) seemed to advance straight to Fulfil, which would have meant the fix did nothing. Before concluding that, checked `maybeAdvanceJourneyStage.toString().includes('Scratch & Win')` directly in the browser console and got `false` — the tab was still running a cached pre-fix copy of `js/session.js` from an earlier test round on the same reused port, a known caching gotcha in this project (`DEVELOPER_HANDOVER.md`, testing-methodology section). Restarted on a fresh port, confirmed the new code was actually loaded, and re-ran the exact same scenario — passed cleanly. Worth restating for whoever picks this up next: a same-port reload is not a reliable way to verify a `js/*.js` change in this environment; use a fresh port (or an explicit cache-busting fetch) every time, and confirm the loaded function source directly if a result looks surprising before trusting it as a real bug.

## 62. Diego's Event Store Panel Was Never Wired Up, Despite His Wizard Already Firing Segment

User question: does `investment-application.html` call Segment? Confirmed yes — same `enableSegmentAnalytics()` pattern as `sip-application.html` (Aria) and `new-application.html` (the loan customers), firing `analytics.identify()`/`analytics.page()` only at the wizard's Confirmation step (stage 2), whether Diego finishes or abandons. Follow-up: why didn't the dashboard's Event Store panel show it?

**Root cause**: `dashboard.html`'s `EVENT_STORE_CUSTOMER_IDS` (`= [...NEW_APPLICATION_CUSTOMER_IDS, "CUST101"]`, i.e. Sam/Maya/Robert/Aria) gates both whether the panel renders at all and whether `loadSupabaseEvents()` ever gets called — Diego was never added to it, even though §33/56's investment wizard already fires the exact Segment calls that panel is meant to read back. Not a bug in the Segment/Supabase wiring itself, just a customer list nobody had updated for a surface that didn't exist yet when that list was written.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: added `"C1004"` to `EVENT_STORE_CUSTOMER_IDS`, kept separate from `NEW_APPLICATION_CUSTOMER_IDS` for the same reason Aria already is — that list also gates the unrelated New Application (loan) card, which Diego isn't offered either.

**Verified live**: reloaded Diego's dashboard fresh (forced, to rule out this session's known stale-cache gotcha) and the Event Store panel now renders — populated with five real "Investment Account Opened" rows, correctly timestamped from this session's own earlier `investment-application.html` test runs (§56, §59, §61), confirming the Segment→Supabase pipeline had been capturing his events correctly all along; only the dashboard's own read-back panel was missing. No console errors.

## 63. A Still-In-Progress Mission's "Track Your Progress" Card Also Got Wiped by a Stage Advance

Bug report: "still seeing only one Track your Progress even though both missions are active." Reproduced with several scenarios before finding the exact trigger — every combination tried where Personalized Offers had already been through a fresh Earn-stage fetch with both missions' state accounted for rendered correctly (both a Track Your Progress and a Mission Completed/other Track Your Progress together). The actual repro needed the SAME stage-advance path §57 fixed for Mission Completed cards, but for a card that's still genuinely pending, not complete: register and fully complete Bank More with Us, register Spread the Word too (merging in its own "Track Your Progress for Spread the Word" at 0%, correctly, per §55) but never touch its one remaining action (Refer Your Buddy) — then accept Bank More's "Mission Completed." That advances Earn straight to Scratch & Win, and §57's own re-merge only put the *other mission's Mission Completed* back if it existed — it never considered a still-in-progress Track Your Progress card, which was just as much a casualty of the same `loadOffers()` wholesale-replace.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: in `handleRespond`'s stage-advanced branch, alongside §57's `getDiegoNewlyCompletedMissionOffers` re-merge, added a second re-merge calling `getDiegoTrackProgressOfferForMission` (§55) for both mission titles — whichever one (if either) is still registered-but-incomplete gets its Track Your Progress card put back, using the same existing function, just now also called from this second call site.

**Verified live**: registered and completed Bank More with Us only, registered Spread the Word (confirmed its Track Your Progress card merged in correctly at that point), then accepted Bank More's Mission Completed — Personalized Offers correctly kept "Track your Progress for Spread the Word" visible alongside the two newly-unlocked scratch cards, instead of losing it. Continued the flow through to completion (Refer Your Buddy → Mission Completed Spread the Word → all three scratch cards → Fulfil, per §61's advance rule) with no further issues and no console errors at any step.

## 64. New "Progress Monitor" Panel Replaces Track Your Progress in Personalized Offers — Retires §54/§55/§57/§63's Whole Bug Class; "Available Missions"/"Available Milestones" Renamed

Direct request: move Track Your Progress out of Personalized Offers into its own standing "Progress Monitor" panel, one that keeps showing a mission's progress (at 100%) even after it completes — plus rename "Available Missions" → "Pending Missions" and "Available Milestones" → "Pending Milestones".

**This isn't just a UI move — it retires an entire recurring bug class.** Every fix from §54 onward that touched Track Your Progress (§54's hide-on-complete rule, §55's cross-stage merge for late registration, §63's cross-stage merge for a still-in-progress mission) existed only because Track Your Progress lived inside Personalized Offers' stage-scoped, merge-and-reconcile machinery — the same machinery Mission Completed, the scratch cards, and their rewards all still need, and the repeated source of "wrong stage, silently wiped, re-merge it back in" bugs throughout this session. Progress Monitor sidesteps all of it: it isn't tied to any stage's fetch at all, has no accept/dismiss action to respond to, and is recomputed fresh from mission state (`journeyActionResponses`) on every single response — the exact same "always current, never stale" shape `getDiegoAvailableMissions`/`getDiegoAvailableMilestones` already use.

**Fix, `js/cdh.js`**: replaced `getDiegoTrackProgressOfferForMission` (§55, now unused) with `getDiegoProgressMonitor(session, customer)` — returns one entry per mission Diego has ever registered for (`journeyActionResponses[mission] === "accepted"`), regardless of completion state, each carrying its live progress percent. `applyPriorityBankingCampaignAdjustments`'s Earn-stage branch was simplified to match: `Track Your Progress`/`Track Your Progress for Spread the Word` are now unconditionally excluded from Personalized Offers (no more registration/completion-dependent filter, no more `autoResolve` calls for them — Earn's own stage-advancement rule never depended on their jar state anyway), leaving only the Mission Completed gating that was already there.

**Fix, `js/render-shared.js`**: `renderOfferCard` gained an opt-in `readOnly` flag — when set, `actionsHtml` renders empty (no Accept/Not Now buttons) instead of going through the normal fallback/apply-now/generic-accept branches, since a monitor entry isn't an offer to act on.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: added `dashState.progressMonitor`, recomputed everywhere `availableMissions`/`availableMilestones` already are (initial load, and unconditionally on every C1004 response in `handleRespond`) via `getDiegoProgressMonitor`. Removed, as dead code now that Track Your Progress no longer enters `dashState.offers` at all: the `wasAvailableMission` block's Track Your Progress merge (§55), the "refresh Track Your Progress in place" filter/map block (§50/§54), and the stage-advanced branch's Track Your Progress re-merge (§63) — none of it has anything left to do. New panel rendered right after Pending Milestones, using `renderOfferCard(..., { readOnly: true })`. "Available Missions"/"Available Milestones" panel titles and their empty-state copy renamed to "Pending Missions"/"Pending Milestones" (internal variable/function names — `availableMissions`, `getDiegoAvailableMissions`, etc. — deliberately left as-is; only user-facing text changed).

**Verified live**, continuous session with no reloads between steps once past the initial setup: registered both missions — Personalized Offers correctly showed only the two Engage actions (no Track Your Progress clutter at all), Progress Monitor correctly showed both at their live percentages (50%/0%) with no Accept/Not Now buttons. Completed Bank More with Us — Personalized Offers cleanly showed just "Mission Completed" (nothing else to reconcile), Progress Monitor showed it at 100%. Accepted Mission Completed (stage advanced to Scratch & Win) — Progress Monitor's 100% entry for Bank More correctly **stayed exactly as it was**, no merge needed to keep it there, unlike every prior Track-Your-Progress fix in this session. Ran the rest of the flow through to Fulfil (completing Spread the Word, scratching all three cards) with both Progress Monitor entries ending at 100% and no other regressions — Pending Missions/Milestones panels, scratch reveals, ordering, and the Scratch & Win advancement rule all still correct. Confirmed the Decision Receipt toggle still works on a read-only Progress Monitor card (no crash, correct explainability panel). No console errors at any point.

## 65. Diego's Dashboard: Smaller Hero Card, Four Panels Now Adjacent in One Row

Direct layout request: shrink the hero (balance) card and place Personalized Offers, Pending Missions, Pending Milestones, and Progress Monitor next to each other rather than stacked in the narrow 340px side column.

**Scope**: entirely conditional on `customer.id === "C1004"` (a new `isDiego` flag computed once per render) — every other customer's dashboard (Robert, Aria, Sam, Maya) keeps the exact original two-column layout, confirmed unaffected by direct comparison (see verification below).

**Fix, `css/app.css`**:
- `.bnk-dashboard-grid--full` — collapses the `minmax(0,1fr) 340px` two-column grid to a single full-width column, freeing the width the 340px side column would otherwise reserve.
- `.bnk-hero-account--compact` — caps the hero card at `max-width: 420px` (it had no max-width before, so on a full-width single-column layout it would otherwise stretch edge-to-edge) — reset back to full width under the existing 860px breakpoint, matching how the hero already behaves on narrow viewports for every other customer.
- `.bnk-diego-panel-row` — a 4-column CSS grid (`repeat(4, minmax(0,1fr))`) holding the four panels side by side, collapsing to 2 columns at ≤860px and 1 column at ≤480px, mirroring the responsive pattern already used for `.bnk-dashboard-grid` and `.bnk-offers-grid`.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: extracted the Personalized Offers, Pending Missions, Pending Milestones, and Progress Monitor panels' markup into four template-string variables (`personalizedOffersPanelHtml`, `pendingMissionsPanelHtml`, `pendingMilestonesPanelHtml`, `progressMonitorPanelHtml`), built once per render so the exact same markup can be dropped into whichever layout applies rather than duplicated. For Diego, `.bnk-side-col` no longer renders at all (nothing left to put there) and the four panels render together, after the grid, inside `.bnk-diego-panel-row`; every other customer keeps `.bnk-side-col` with just Personalized Offers in it, byte-identical to before this change.

**Verified live**: confirmed via direct DOM inspection on Diego's dashboard — `.bnk-diego-panel-row` exists with exactly 4 children (`Personalized Offers`, `Pending Missions`, `Pending Milestones`, `Progress Monitor`, in that order), `grid-template-columns` computed to 4 equal columns, `.bnk-side-col` absent, hero card carries `bnk-hero-account--compact` and measured 420px wide (capped, as intended). Ran a live interaction through it (registering a mission) to confirm the row's panels still update correctly, not just render once. Then switched to Robert's dashboard on the same server and confirmed the reverse: `.bnk-diego-panel-row` absent, `.bnk-side-col` present, hero card plain (no compact class, measured 688px — its original, unconstrained width) — zero effect on his layout. No console errors on either customer.

## 66. §65 Follow-Up: Hero Card Moved Into the Same Row as the Four Panels, Not Just Above It

Immediate refinement of §65: the hero card and the four panels sat in separate rows (hero above, full-width panel row below) — the request was for the hero card to sit directly *beside* the four panels, all adjacent in one row.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: extracted the hero card's own markup into a `heroCardHtml` template-string variable (same pattern as the four panel variables), built once per render. For Diego, `.bnk-diego-panel-row` now holds all five — `heroCardHtml` first, then the four panels — replacing `.bnk-dashboard-grid` entirely rather than sitting inside a separate row above it; everything else that used to live in `.bnk-main-col` alongside the hero (New Application card, Event Store, Brevo card, Activity ledger) now sits in its own plain `.bnk-main-col` below this row instead. Every other customer keeps using `.bnk-dashboard-grid` exactly as before (hero back inside `.bnk-main-col`, `heroCardHtml` unchanged either way), so nothing about their layout moved.

**Fix, `css/app.css`**: `.bnk-diego-panel-row` is now a 5-column grid — `300px repeat(4, minmax(0,1fr))` — instead of 4; `.bnk-hero-account--compact`'s cap tightened from 420px to 300px to match the grid column it now sits inside (the grid's explicit column width already constrains it, but keeping the two in sync avoids a mismatched intent if either changes independently later). The existing 860px/480px responsive collapses (§65) needed no changes — they already reduce the row to 2 columns then 1, which now naturally includes the hero card as just another cell in the grid rather than a separate element to handle.

**Verified live**: `.bnk-diego-panel-row` now has exactly 5 children in order (hero card, Personalized Offers, Pending Missions, Pending Milestones, Progress Monitor), hero's own class list still carries `bnk-hero-account--compact`. Screenshotted at 1600px width — hero card and all four panels visibly sit in one row, side by side. Confirmed the row is still live (registering a mission updated Progress Monitor's cell in place). Checked Aria's dashboard on the same server: unchanged, hero card full-width, standard side column with just Personalized Offers — zero effect on any other customer. No console errors on either.

## 68. Diego's Layout, Take Three: Hero Card Back at the Top (Original Width, Shortened Height), All Four Panels Always Together Below It

Direct feedback after §65–§67's row-based experiments ("This is not looking good") led to a reset, then two more rounds of direct steering:

1. Hero card moves back to the TOP of the page, using its ORIGINAL width (not narrowed like §65/§66/§67's `max-width` cap) — shortened only in *height*.
2. Below it, one full-width row holds Pending Missions, Pending Milestones, Progress Monitor, and Personalized Offers, with Personalized Offers last.
3. First pass: the three status panels were shown only when they actually had data. User feedback: not wanted — reverted immediately to always showing all four, side by side, no conditional hiding.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: `heroCardHtml` no longer carries any width constraint from `isDiego` — it renders as a plain top-level block (full width) directly in `.bnk-body`, above `.bnk-diego-panel-row`, rather than being one of the row's grid items. `.bnk-diego-panel-row` always includes all four panels unconditionally, in fixed order (missions, milestones, progress, personalized-last).

**Fix, `css/app.css`**: `.bnk-hero-account--compact` was repurposed from a width cap (§65's `max-width: 420px`, tightened to 300px in §67) to a set of vertical spacing reductions only — tighter padding, smaller gaps between the top row/label/masked/balance rows, smaller action-button icons (26px → 20px) and labels, tighter subaccount spacing — while leaving every original element in place (chip, type/active pill, label, masked number, balance + toggle, all four action buttons, both subaccount rows). Measured height dropped from 259px (a plain, uncompacted hero, e.g. Aria's) to 224px (~13.5% shorter) at the same viewport width. `.bnk-diego-panel-row` uses `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr))` — adapts to viewport width on its own, so the manual 860px/480px breakpoint overrides from §65 (which assumed a fixed column count) were removed as dead code.

**Verified live**: confirmed hero card renders as a top-level, non-narrowed element (full width, same as every other customer's) with the `--compact` class only affecting its internal spacing; measured its height directly (224px vs. 259px baseline). Confirmed `.bnk-diego-panel-row` always contains all four panels in the correct order — checked on both a fresh session (no missions registered, panels showing their empty-state copy) and after registering both missions (panels showing live data) — per the explicit "no need to regression test" instruction, this round skipped the cross-customer non-Diego verification other §-fixes in this session included. No console errors in either state checked.

## 70. Pending Missions/Milestones and Progress Monitor: Equal Height, Light Blue Background

Direct request off the §69 screenshot: align the height of the three status panels with each other, and give them a background color instead of white.

**Equal height without dragging Personalized Offers along**: the four panels share one flex row (`.bnk-diego-panel-row`), and naively adding `align-items: stretch` there would have matched all four to whichever is tallest — usually Personalized Offers, whose height varies wildly (zero offers to several real cards), which would have left the three status panels awkwardly tall with dead space whenever Offers was short, or too short to matter whenever Offers was tall. Fixed by wrapping just the three status panels in their own inner grid, `.bnk-diego-status-panels` (`display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(180px,1fr)); align-items:stretch`), sitting as ONE flex item inside `.bnk-diego-panel-row` alongside Personalized Offers — so the three stretch to match only each other, while Personalized Offers keeps its own independent height entirely.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: added a `bnk-status-panel` class (alongside the existing `bnk-sidebar-panel`) to just the three status panels' markup — not Personalized Offers — so CSS could target them specifically without a new wrapper-scoped approach.

**Fix, `css/app.css`**: `.bnk-sidebar-panel.bnk-status-panel` (two classes, so it reliably wins over the shared `.bnk-sidebar-panel`'s white background regardless of stylesheet source order) sets `background: var(--brass-soft)` — the same light blue already used for icon badges/pills elsewhere in the app — plus `display:flex; flex-direction:column`, with `.bnk-sidebar-offers-stack` given `flex:1` and `.bnk-sidebar-note` given `margin-top:auto`, so a stretched-taller panel's content grows and its note anchors to the bottom rather than leaving an obviously empty gap in the middle. Also caught and fixed a color collision this same change introduced: `.bnk-panel-empty-icon`'s circle (§69) was `background: var(--brass-soft)` too — identical to the panel's new background, which would have made the icon invisible — changed to `var(--white)` for contrast against the now-blue panel.

**Verified live**: measured all three `.bnk-status-panel` elements' heights directly — identical (269px in the empty state, 666px after registering a mission and populating real content) — confirming the stretch works correctly in both states, not just the empty one. Confirmed background color `rgb(219,234,254)` (`--brass-soft`) on all three, `rgb(255,255,255)` (unchanged) on Personalized Offers. No console errors.

## 71. Personalized Offers Deduplicated Against Pending Missions/Milestones; Third Scratch Card Gets a "Fulfillment Info" Button

Two requests. First: an offer showing in Pending Missions or Pending Milestones shouldn't *also* show in Personalized Offers — a real duplicate, not a display glitch. Root cause: while Diego is actually ON the Register (or Engage) stage himself, that stage's own current fetch legitimately contains the exact same titles `getDiegoAvailableMissions`/`getDiegoAvailableMilestones` independently compute (both missions, while still unregistered, are simultaneously "this stage's own action" and "available to register") — two different code paths correctly returning the same content, rendered in two different panels.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: for Diego specifically, `offersToShow` (what Personalized Offers actually renders) now filters out any offer whose normalized title already appears in `dashState.availableMissions` or `dashState.availableMilestones`, before rendering. Pending Missions/Milestones keep showing everything they always did — only Personalized Offers' copy gets excluded.

Second: give the third scratch card a dedicated "Fulfillment Info" button, and make clicking it hand Diego cleanly off to the Fulfil stage — hiding the scratched card rather than leaving its reveal sitting next to Fulfil's own two offers the way Cards 1/2's reveals do.

**Fix, `js/cdh.js`**: `DIEGO_CTA_OVERRIDES`'s entry for "Your Third Scratch Card, Looks Like You're a Pro" changed from `"Scratch"` to `"Fulfillment Info"` — it's the only one of the three cards whose acceptance can carry Diego out of Scratch & Win entirely (§61's advancement rule), so its button now reads as what it actually leads to.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: in `handleRespond`'s stage-advanced branch, the §59 scratch-reveal-append (which pushes the just-scratched card, revealed, onto whichever stage the response advanced into) now explicitly excludes Card 3 by title — so completing the flow via "Fulfillment Info" shows Fulfil's own two offers alone, while Card 1/2's reveal-in-place behavior (a separate code path, unaffected) stays exactly as it was. If Card 3 is scratched *before* the other prerequisites are met (stage doesn't advance yet), it still reveals in place like the other two — this exclusion only applies to the actual completing click.

**Verified live**, one continuous flow: registered both missions — confirmed Personalized Offers showed nothing (both titles correctly deduplicated away, Register-stage panel legitimately empty) while Pending Missions showed both, unaffected. Completed both missions and scratched Cards 1 and 2 — confirmed Card 3 showed the new "Fulfillment Info" label. Clicked it — `currentStageIndex` advanced to 5 (Fulfil), and Personalized Offers showed exactly the two Fulfil offers, no lingering Card 3 anywhere. No console errors at any step.

## 72. Fulfilment Offers Stay Visible After Response, "View Details" CTA, Illustrated Empty State for Personalized Offers

Three requests. First: responding to either Fulfil-stage offer shouldn't remove it from Personalized Offers — it's the last stage of the journey, so unlike every earlier response (which clears a card to make room for what's next), there's nothing further for it to make room for. Second: their CTA should read "View Details" instead of the generic "View offer". Third: Personalized Offers looked bland with plain italic text when Diego has nothing to show — same complaint §69 already fixed for the three status panels, now extended to this panel too.

**Fix, `js/cdh.js`**: added `"View Details"` to `DIEGO_CTA_OVERRIDES` for both Fulfil titles. Added `DIEGO_FULFIL_TITLES`, a small normalized-title array reused by `dashboard.html`.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: `handleRespond`'s "stage didn't change" branch now checks `DIEGO_FULFIL_TITLES` before its normal "remove the responded offer from view" step — a Fulfil-stage offer skips that removal entirely, so `responses[offer.id]` (already set by `respondToOffer`) drives `renderOfferCard`'s own "Accepted"/"Dismissed" badge instead of the card disappearing. Also added the same `.bnk-panel-empty` illustrated empty state (§69) to Personalized Offers' own two empty-state branches (`dashState.offers.length === 0`, and post-dedup `offersToShow.length === 0`), scoped to `isDiegoJourney` only — every other customer's Personalized Offers panel keeps the original plain `bnk-journey-empty` text, unchanged.

**Fix, `css/app.css`**: caught a contrast bug while wiring the empty-state illustration into Personalized Offers — `.bnk-panel-empty-icon` was `background: var(--white)` (per §70, so it would show up against the blue `.bnk-status-panel` background), but Personalized Offers' own panel background is white, so a white icon circle there would have been invisible. Fixed by flipping the default back to brass-soft (correct for Personalized Offers' white panel) and adding a `.bnk-status-panel .bnk-panel-empty-icon` override back to white specifically for the three status panels — each context now gets the icon color that actually contrasts with its own panel background.

**Verified live**: confirmed the empty-state illustration renders on Personalized Offers with a brass-soft icon circle, while the status panels' icons independently confirmed still white — no cross-contamination between the two contexts. Drove the full flow to Fulfil stage: both offers correctly showed "View Details". Accepted one, dismissed the other — both stayed visible with "Accepted"/"Dismissed" badges respectively instead of disappearing. No console errors at any step.

## 73. Personalized Offers Empty-State Copy Reworded

Follow-up to §72: "No personalized offers available right now" read as if something were broken, especially now that §71's dedup means this state triggers fairly routinely (e.g. the moment Diego reaches Register stage, before registering either mission — both titles are legitimately duplicates of Pending Missions and get filtered out of Personalized Offers entirely). Reworded to explain *why* it's empty rather than just stating that it is.

**Fix, `dashboard.html`**: `diegoOffersEmptyHtml`'s text changed to "You're all caught up here — new offers will appear as you complete missions and milestones." Single shared constant (§72), so both of Personalized Offers' empty-state branches (pre- and post-dedup) picked up the new copy automatically — no other change needed.

**Verified live**: reached Register stage fresh (both missions still duplicated away per §71) and confirmed the panel now shows the new message. No console errors.
